**Conversation Transcript & Support Resolution:**
- **[18:34, 01/08/2026] Student:** Buen día, me llego un correo para extender prácticas
- **[12:52, 03/08/2026] Animafest Support:** Primero tienes que hablar con tu hotel para saber si están de acuerdo en prorrogar las prácticas, luego con tu escuela para saber si están de acuerdo en volver a firmar el convenio, luego tienes que comprar de nuevo el seguro privado y renovar los documentos que tengan más de 3 meses, El proceso es más fácil porque ya estás en España (proceso de los 7 documentos). si tienes la oportunidad para quedarte en el mismo lugar entonces necesitas: - Escanear su pasaporte con visado que llegaste - comprar el seguro para viaje - rellenar correctamente el documento EX-04 indicando que se trata de una prórroga - firmar con tu escuela el nuevo contrato -Escribanos también que fechas exactas necesitas Si tu hotel no está de acuerdo podemos buscar otra práctica.
- **[12:57, 03/08/2026] Student:** Ya no es necesario que se pida el certificado de estudios cierto?
- **[14:54, 03/08/2026] Animafest Support:** ¿No vas a ser un estudiante activo?
- **[14:54, 03/08/2026] Animafest Support:** ¿Te vas a graduar pronto?

Hello, unfortunately not Why Can't I use the other documents since they are still valid Hi Moreen! Documents must be legalized in the country that issued them because Spanish authorities can't verify foreign stamps locally. Also, immigration requires fresh, updated documents (like criminal records, which usually expire after 90 days) and won't accept previously used ones. The best option is to ask someone back home to help obtain fresh copies and legalize them for you!

- **[16:43, 05/08/2026] Animafest Support:** First, you need to speak with your hotel to see if they agree to extend the internship, then with your school to find out if they agree to sign the agreement again. After that, you have to purchase private insurance again and renew any documents that are older than 3 months. The process is easier since you're already in Spain (7-document process). If you have the opportunity to stay in the same place, then you need to: - Scan your passport with the visa you arrived with - Purchase travel insurance - Properly fill out the EX-04 form, indicating that it's an extension - Sign the new contract with your school -Write us also exact dates that you need If your hotel doesn't agree, we can look for another internship.

### Case #34: Student Amie 121502
**Context / Participant:** Amie 121502
**Conversation Transcript & Support Resolution:**
- **[19:13, 15/06/2026] Student:** Yes I want to extend
- **[09:29, 16/06/2026] Animafest Support:** First, you need to speak with your hotel to see if they agree to extend the internship, then with your school to find out if they agree to sign the agreement again. After that, you have to purchase private insurance again and renew any documents that are older than 3 months. The process is easier since you're already in Spain (7-document process). If you have the opportunity to stay in the same place, then you need to: - Scan your passport with the visa you arrived with - Purchase travel insurance - Properly fill out the EX-04 form, indicating that it's an extension - Sign the new contract with your school -Write us also exact dates that you need If your hotel doesn't agree, we can look for another internship.
- **[11:07, 24/06/2026] Animafest Support:** Dear Amie We would like to inform you about the arrival to the hotel which company passed to us, Of course if you already there you can delete the message WHEN YOU LEAVE THE TERMINAL WITH YOUR SUITCASE, SPEAK TO THE FROM2 REPRESENTATIVE (WEARING AN ORANGE T-SHIRT) WHO IS WAITING RIGHT NEXT TO THE TOURIST OFFICE. THEY WILL INFORM YOU OF THE BUS DEPARTURE TIME. DO NOT LEAVE THE TERMINAL WITHOUT SPEAKING TO THE REPRESENTATIVE Regards Animafest
- **[11:28, 24/06/2026] Student:** Greetings dear I am already in Spain since May 11 and was asking about extending my internship
- **[12:30, 24/06/2026] Animafest Support:** First, you need to speak with your hotel to see if they agree to extend the internship, then with your school to find out if they agree to sign the agreement again. After that, you have to purchase private insurance again and renew any documents that are older than 3 months. The process is easier since you're already in Spain (7-document process). If you have the opportunity to stay in the same place, then you need to: - Scan your passport with the visa you arrived with - Purchase travel insurance - Properly fill out the EX-04 form, indicating that it's an extension - Sign the new contract with your school -Write us also exact dates that you need If your hotel doesn't agree, we can look for another internship.

- **[14:16, 16/07/2026] Animafest Support:** First, you need to speak with your hotel to see if they agree to extend the internship, then with your school to find out if they agree to sign the agreement again. After that, you have to purchase private insurance again and renew any documents that are older than 3 months. The process is easier since you're already in Spain (7-document process). If you have the opportunity to stay in the same place, then you need to: - Scan your passport with the visa you arrived with - Purchase travel insurance - Properly fill out the EX-04 form, indicating that it's an extension - Sign the new contract with your school -Write us also exact dates that you need If your hotel doesn't agree, we can look for another internship.

- **[13:57, 03/07/2026] Animafest Support:** First, you need to speak with your hotel to see if they agree to extend the internship, then with your school to find out if they agree to sign the agreement again. After that, you have to purchase private insurance again and renew any documents that are older than 3 months. The process is easier since you're already in Spain (7-document process). If you have the opportunity to stay in the same place, then you need to: - Scan your passport with the visa you arrived with - Purchase travel insurance - Properly fill out the EX-04 form, indicating that it's an extension - Sign the new contract with your school -Write us also exact dates that you need If your hotel doesn't agree, we can look for another internship.

- **[09:24, 03/07/2026] Animafest Support:** First, you need to speak with your hotel to see if they agree to extend the internship, then with your school to find out if they agree to sign the agreement again. After that, you have to purchase private insurance again and renew any documents that are older than 3 months. The process is easier since you're already in Spain (7-document process). If you have the opportunity to stay in the same place, then you need to: - Scan your passport with the visa you arrived with - Purchase travel insurance - Properly fill out the EX-04 form, indicating that it's an extension - Sign the new contract with your school -Write us also exact dates that you need If your hotel doesn't agree, we can look for another internship.

### Case #154: Student Aixa 118792
**Context / Participant:** Aixa 118792
**Conversation Transcript & Support Resolution:**
- **[14:42, 29/07/2026] Student:** Buenas, quisiera consultar si en caso de querer extender las prácticas tiene que ser un periodo de 6 meses si o si o podría ser menos tiempo. Muchas gracias
- **[16:46, 29/07/2026] Animafest Support:** Hola, sí, tiene que ser de al menos 6 meses.
- **[08:53, 14/08/2026] Student:** Buenos días, quisiera información acerca de la extensión de prácticas. Gracias
- **[08:54, 14/08/2026] Student:** Buenos días, quisiera información acerca de la extensión de prácticas. Gracias
- **[11:00, 14/08/2026] Animafest Support:** Primero tienes que hablar con tu hotel para saber si están de acuerdo en prorrogar las prácticas, luego con tu escuela para saber si están de acuerdo en volver a firmar el convenio, luego tienes que comprar de nuevo el seguro privado y renovar los documentos que tengan más de 3 meses, El proceso es más fácil porque ya estás en España (proceso de los 7 documentos). si tienes la oportunidad para quedarte en el mismo lugar entonces necesitas: - Escanear su pasaporte con visado que llegaste - comprar el seguro para viaje - rellenar correctamente el documento EX-04 indicando que se trata de una prórroga - firmar con tu escuela el nuevo contrato -Escribanos también que fechas exactas necesitas Si tu hotel no está de acuerdo podemos buscar otra práctica.

Me comunico porque tengo una consulta respecto al proceso de selección. Estoy por renovar mi pasaporte el martes 18 y lo estaré recibiendo dentro de las 96 horas siguientes. Quería saber qué tan importante es tener el número de pasaporte cargado en la plataforma para poder recibir propuestas de hoteles o restaurantes, y si esto puede llegar a demorar o condicionar mi selección. Por otro lado, quería consultarles si es posible recibir orientación sobre el proceso completo, ya que me gustaría ir avanzando con toda la documentación de manera ordenada y sin contratiempos. Quedo atento a su respuesta. ¡Muchas gracias! Mauricio Jiménez

### Case #172: Student Steffany 118597
**Context / Participant:** Steffany 118597
**Conversation Transcript & Support Resolution:**
- **[13:15, 10/08/2026] Student:** Hola, quiero extender mi practica
- **[13:16, 10/08/2026] Student:** Que se debe de necesitar?
- **[16:50, 10/08/2026] Animafest Support:** Primero tienes que hablar con tu hotel para saber si están de acuerdo en prorrogar las prácticas, luego con tu escuela para saber si están de acuerdo en volver a firmar el convenio, luego tienes que comprar de nuevo el seguro privado y renovar los documentos que tengan más de 3 meses, El proceso es más fácil porque ya estás en España (proceso de los 7 documentos). si tienes la oportunidad para quedarte en el mismo lugar entonces necesitas: - Escanear su pasaporte con visado que llegaste - comprar el seguro para viaje - rellenar correctamente el documento EX-04 indicando que se trata de una prórroga - firmar con tu escuela el nuevo contrato -Escribanos también que fechas exactas necesitas Si tu hotel no está de acuerdo podemos buscar otra práctica.

- **[20:18, 13/08/2026] Student:** O sea cierra en septiembre

- **[10:14, 14/08/2026] Student:** Yo las empecé
- **[10:14, 14/08/2026] Student:** El 8 de junio
- **[10:14, 14/08/2026] Student:** Quiero alargarlas otro par de meses
- **[11:02, 14/08/2026] Animafest Support:** ¿Seis meses?
- **[11:04, 14/08/2026] Student:** Sería 3 meses más
- **[12:01, 14/08/2026] Animafest Support:** Gracias por la información. Le enviaré tu perfil a mi director.
- **[16:08, 14/08/2026] Student:** Gracias, o si me gustaría los 6 meses

- **[16:08, 14/08/2026] Student:** Pues la idea es que quiero alargar las prácticas

- **[16:52, 14/08/2026] Animafest Support:** First, you need to speak with your hotel to see if they agree to extend the internship, then with your school to find out if they agree to sign the agreement again. After that, you have to purchase private insurance again and renew any documents that are older than 3 months. The process is easier since you're already in Spain (7-document process). If you have the opportunity to stay in the same place, then you need to: - Scan your passport with the visa you arrived with - Purchase travel insurance - Properly fill out the EX-04 form, indicating that it's an extension - Sign the new contract with your school -Write us also exact dates that you need If your hotel doesn't agree, we can look for another internship.
- **[18:59, 14/08/2026] Student:** Okay

Tus prácticas serán CANCELADAS si la documentación sigue incompleta y no dejas ninguna respuesta Querido estudiante Alan escríbenos hoy mismo para evitar la cancelación 💫 ¿Preguntas? ¡Estamos aquí para ayudarte! Saludos Animafest

- **[14:40, 10/06/2026] Animafest Support:** Primero tienes que hablar con tu hotel para saber si están de acuerdo en prorrogar las prácticas, luego con tu escuela para saber si están de acuerdo en volver a firmar el convenio, luego tienes que comprar de nuevo el seguro privado y renovar los documentos que tengan más de 3 meses, El proceso es más fácil porque ya estás en España (proceso de los 7 documentos). si tienes la oportunidad para quedarte en el mismo lugar entonces necesitas: - Escanear su pasaporte con visado que llegaste - comprar el seguro para viaje - rellenar correctamente el documento EX-04 indicando que se trata de una prórroga - firmar con tu escuela el nuevo contrato -Escribanos también que fechas exactas necesitas Si tu hotel no está de acuerdo podemos buscar otra práctica.

- **[10:00, 12/08/2026] Animafest Support:** Además, en tu formulario ex-04 tienes que marcar estas casillas porque tienes una prórroga.
- **[10:03, 12/08/2026] Animafest Support:** Además, dado que tienes una prórroga, tu pasaporte debe llevar un sello de entrada a España.

- **[14:05, 31/07/2026] Animafest Support:** First, you need to speak with your hotel to see if they agree to extend the internship, then with your school to find out if they agree to sign the agreement again. After that, you have to purchase private insurance again and renew any documents that are older than 3 months. The process is easier since you're already in Spain (7-document process). If you have the opportunity to stay in the same place, then you need to: - Scan your passport with the visa you arrived with - Purchase travel insurance - Properly fill out the EX-04 form, indicating that it's an extension - Sign the new contract with your school -Write us also exact dates that you need If your hotel doesn't agree, we can look for another internship.

- **[23:45, 17/08/2026] Animafest Support:** ¡Hola, María! Espero que las prácticas de cocina en el Hotel Tropic te estén yendo muy bien. 😃 ¿Te está gustando tanto como para querer quedarte más tiempo? ¡Dímelo y lo organizamos! ¡Hola, María! Espero que las prácticas de cocina en el Hotel Tropic te estén yendo muy bien. ¿Te está gustando tanto como para querer quedarte más tiempo? ¡Dímelo y lo organizamos! Hola, me gustaría saber el proceso para extender el convenio Vale, lo primero que necesito saber es si te gustaría prolongar tu estancia en el mismo hotel o en otro sitio. - **[15:59, 10/08/2026] Animafest Support:** First, you need to speak with your hotel to see if they agree to extend the internship, then with your school to find out if they agree to sign the agreement again. After that, you have to purchase private insurance again and renew any documents that are older than 3 months. The process is easier since you're already in Spain (7-document process). If you have the opportunity to stay in the same place, then you need to: - Scan your passport with the visa you arrived with - Purchase travel insurance - Properly fill out the EX-04 form, indicating that it's an extension - Sign the new contract with your school -Write us also exact dates that you need If your hotel doesn't agree, we can look for another internship.
